Title: Codex Constitutionum Ordinis S. Basilii M. ex sancitis antiquiorum Capitulorum Congregationis Ruthenorum et Decretorum Sanctae Sedis collectarum, ad textum Regulae S. Patris nostri exactus et conformatus
Creator: The Ruthenian Basilian Order (Congregation of the Ruthenians of the Order of St Basil the Great); no individual compiler named on the title page
Publisher: Typis S. Reg. M. Monasterii Poczajoviensis O. S. B. M., 1791 (the Basilian press at Pochaiv)
Publication Date: 1791
Rights: Public Domain (published 1791, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item's own rights statement reads 'Domena publiczna (public domain)')
Language: lat
Source: Internet Archive item jbc.bj.uj.edu.pl.NDIGSTDR024052 (https://archive.org/details/jbc.bj.uj.edu.pl.NDIGSTDR024052), the item's own NDIGSTDR024052_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: Biblioteka Jagiellońska; host metadata date: 1791-01-26. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: 9cd3e8d7cc0018974ca196365f8efc9a967359a53d32c1bb6742d19ef2e92f33.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. Jagiellonian Library (Biblioteka Jagiellońska) scan, mirrored to the host in the Polish public-domain collection with Tesseract OCR; the library marks it public domain. The title page imprint reads '1791. Anno.' and the press 'Monasterii Poczajoviensis'.
Content note: The constitutions of the Ruthenian Basilian order as fixed from its chapters and the Holy See's decrees, opening with a prolegomenon from St Basil's ascetic teaching; the rule of life of the order that ran the Uniate church's monasteries, schools and episcopate.
